On the Anniversary Cup page, in the start date info, accompanied by confetti when opening the page  3. On Banana Joe’s page, in front of the Joe Jr. family tie display  4. On the Tournaments page, in the featured tournament block  5. On any agent’s page, in the header of the players block  6. In the title bar of your Team Hall of Fame page  7. On any game preview page, in the coaches block  8. On the Version History page, next to the text “Anniversary Cup”  9. On the results page of any Banana Day game, next to the final score  10. [2025-10-19 21:39] led.lennon.atleticogranata : Guys, welcome to everyone that wants to develop a script. I'm not against the use of scripts to get more information from the game, provided that this is limited to a fair quantity.  The first limitation is in the issues you may generate querying the database using inefficient calls that, for example, are made to query a single player. Please Don't.  You may disrupt the experience of other players, making the website excessively slow or even inaccessible. I'm available to create specific queries on request to make the data collection faster and efficient.  I encourage you all to share your scripts with the community. If your script is particularly efficient and nice, I can integrate it into the game, making it available to be used into the game as, for example, I did it with New Rockers FC script. Every script integrated in the website will be properly acknowledged, and I'm sure people will be grateful for that. Please help to avoid to waste unuseful resource (it means money I need to spend for the servers) and to waste my time in tracking you and your number of queries (I have the track of the number of queries every single user does) and to block your activities. Have fun!
